11505: # --------------------------------------------------
11506: # _validate_pod_code_agreement
11507: #
11508: # Purpose:    Compare POD parameter documentation
11509: #             against code-inferred parameters and
11510: #             return a list of disagreements when
11511: #             strict_pod mode is enabled.
11512: #
11513: # Entry:      $pod_params  - hashref of parameters
11514: #                            from POD analysis.
11515: #             $code_params - hashref of parameters
11516: #                            from code analysis.
11517: #             $method_name - method name string,
11518: #                            used for context in
11519: #                            error messages.
11520: #
11521: # Exit:       Returns a list of disagreement
11522: #             strings. Returns an empty list if
11523: #             all parameters agree.
11524: #
11525: # Side effects: None.
11526: #
11527: # Notes:      Type mismatches are classified as
11528: #             either 'compatible' (e.g. integer vs
11529: #             number) or 'incompatible' via
11530: #             _types_are_compatible. $self and
11531: #             $class are excluded from undocumented
11532: #             parameter warnings in appropriate
11533: #             context.
11534: # --------------------------------------------------
11535: sub _validate_pod_code_agreement {
●11536 → 11543 → 1161411536: 	my ($self, $pod_params, $code_params, $method_name) = @_;
11537: 
11538: 	my @errors;
11539: 
11540: 	# Get all parameter names from both sources
11541: 	my %all_params = map { $_ => 1 } (keys %$pod_params, keys %$code_params);
11542: 
11543: 	foreach my $param (sort keys %all_params) {
11544: 		my $pod = $pod_params->{$param} || {};
11545: 		my $code = $code_params->{$param} || {};
11546: 
11547: 		# A =head3|4 Input formal spec is authoritative for the parameter's
11548: 		# existence, type, optionality and pattern: heuristic code analysis may
11549: 		# legitimately disagree.  Its bounds are still checked below, because a
11550: 		# formal minimum and a code maximum that do not overlap leave the merged
11551: 		# schema with no valid value.
11552: 		unless(($pod->{_source} // '') eq SOURCE_INPUT_SPEC) {
11553: 			# Check if parameter exists in both
11554: 			if (exists $pod_params->{$param} && !exists $code_params->{$param}) {
11555: 				push @errors, "Parameter '\$$param' documented in POD but not found in code signature";
11556: 				next;
11557: 			}
11558: 
11559: 			if(!exists $pod_params->{$param} && exists $code_params->{$param}) {
11560: 				if($param =~ /^(class|pkg|proto|klass)$/i) {
11561: 					# class invocant, not a user-facing parameter
11562: 					next;
11563: 				}
11564: 				if($param eq 'self') {
11565: 					# instance invocant, not a user-facing parameter
11566: 					next;
11567: 				}
11568: 				push @errors, "Parameter '\$$param' found in code but not documented in POD";
11569: 				next;
11570: 			}
11571: 
11572: 			# Compare types if both exist
11573: 			if ($pod->{type} && $code->{type} && $pod->{type} ne $code->{type}) {
11574: 				if (!$self->_types_are_compatible($pod->{type}, $code->{type})) {
11575: 					push @errors, "Type mismatch for '\$$param': POD says '$pod->{type}', code suggests '$code->{type}' (incompatible)";
11576: 				} else {
11577: 					push @errors, "Type difference for '\$$param': POD says '$pod->{type}', code suggests '$code->{type}' (compatible)";
11578: 				}
11579: 			}
11580: 
11581: 			# Compare optional status if both exist
11582: 			if (exists $pod->{optional} && exists $code->{optional} &&
11583: 				$pod->{optional} != $code->{optional}) {
11584: 				my $pod_status = $pod->{optional} ? 'optional' : 'required';
11585: 				my $code_status = $code->{optional} ? 'optional' : 'required';
11586: 				push @errors, "Optional status mismatch for '\$$param': POD says '$pod_status', code suggests '$code_status'";
11587: 			}
11588: 
11589: 			# Check regex patterns
11590: 			if ($pod->{matches} && $code->{matches} && $pod->{matches} ne $code->{matches}) {
11591: 				push @errors, "Pattern mismatch for '\$$param': POD says '$pod->{matches}', code suggests '$code->{matches}'";
11592: 			}
11593: 		}
11594: 
11595: 		# Check constraints (min/max), including a formal spec's bounds.
11596: 		if (defined $pod->{min} && defined $code->{min} && $pod->{min} != $code->{min}) {
11597: 			push @errors, "Min constraint mismatch for '\$$param': POD says '$pod->{min}', code suggests '$code->{min}'";
11598: 		}
11599: 
11600: 		if (defined $pod->{max} && defined $code->{max} && $pod->{max} != $code->{max}) {
11601: 			push @errors, "Max constraint mismatch for '\$$param': POD says '$pod->{max}', code suggests '$code->{max}'";
11602: 		}
11603: 
11604: 		if (defined $pod->{min} && defined $code->{max} && $pod->{min} > $code->{max}) {
11605: 			push @errors, "Constraint contradiction for '\$$param': POD says min '$pod->{min}', code suggests max '$code->{max}' (no valid value)";
11606: 		}
11607: 
11608: 		if (defined $code->{min} && defined $pod->{max} && $code->{min} > $pod->{max}) {
11609: 			push @errors, "Constraint contradiction for '\$$param': code suggests min '$code->{min}', POD says max '$pod->{max}' (no valid value)";
11610: 		}
11611: 	}
11612: 
11613: 	# Return errors (empty array if no errors)
11614: 	return @errors;
11615: }

11617: # --------------------------------------------------
11618: # _validate_strictness_level
11619: #
11620: # Purpose:    Validate and normalise the strict_pod
11621: #             option value accepted by new() into
11622: #             an integer level: 0 (off), 1 (warn),
11623: #             or 2 (fatal).
11624: #
11625: # Entry:      $val - the raw value passed to
11626: #                    strict_pod in new(). May be
11627: #                    undef, a number, or a string.
11628: #
11629: # Exit:       Returns 0, 1, or 2.
11630: #             Croaks if the value is not recognised.
11631: #
11632: # Side effects: None.
11633: # --------------------------------------------------
11634: sub _validate_strictness_level {
11635: 	my $val = $_[0];
11636: 
11637: 	return 0 unless defined $val;
11638: 
11639: 	# Numeric
11640: 	return 0 if $val =~ /^(0|off|none)$/i;
11641: 	return 1 if $val =~ /^(1|warn|warning)$/i;
11642: 	return 2 if $val =~ /^(2|fatal|die|error)$/i;
11643: 
11644: 	croak("Invalid value for --strict-pod: '$val' (use off|warn|fatal)");
11645: }

11647: # --------------------------------------------------
11648: # _types_are_compatible
11649: #
11650: # Purpose:    Determine whether two type strings
11651: #             are compatible for POD/code agreement
11652: #             checking, allowing semantically
11653: #             equivalent types (e.g. 'integer' and
11654: #             'number') to coexist without
11655: #             triggering a strict POD warning.
11656: #
11657: # Entry:      $pod_type  - type string from POD.
11658: #             $code_type - type string from code.
11659: #
11660: # Exit:       Returns 1 if compatible, 0 otherwise.
11661: #
11662: # Side effects: None.
11663: # --------------------------------------------------
11664: sub _types_are_compatible {
●11665 → 11681 → 1168611665: 	my ($self, $pod_type, $code_type) = @_;
11666: 
11667: 	# Exact match is always compatible
11668: 	return 1 if $pod_type eq $code_type;
11669: 
11670: 	# Define compatibility matrix
11671: 	my %compatible_types = (
11672: 		'integer' => ['number', 'scalar'],
11673: 		'number' => ['scalar'],
11674: 		'string' => ['scalar'],
11675: 		'scalar' => ['string', 'integer', 'number'],
11676: 		'arrayref' => ['array'],
11677: 		'hashref' => ['hash'],
11678: 	);
11679: 
11680: 	# Check if code_type is compatible with pod_type
11681: 	if (my $allowed = $compatible_types{$pod_type}) {
11682: 		return grep { $_ eq $code_type } @$allowed;
11683: 	}
11684: 
11685: 	# Check if pod_type is compatible with code_type
●11686 → 11686 → 1169011686: 	if (my $allowed = $compatible_types{$code_type}) {
11687: 		return grep { $_ eq $pod_type } @$allowed;
11688: 	}
11689: 
11690: 	return 0;	# Not compatible
11691: }
11692: 
11693: =head2 generate_pod_validation_report
11694: 
11695: Generate a human-readable report of all POD/code disagreements found
11696: across a set of extracted schemas.
11697: 
11698:     my $schemas = $extractor->extract_all(no_write => 1);
11699:     my $report  = $extractor->generate_pod_validation_report($schemas);
11700:     print $report;
11701: 
11702: =head3 Arguments
11703: 
11704: =over 4
11705: 
11706: =item * C<$schemas>
11707: 
11708: A hashref of method name to schema hashref as returned by
11709: C<extract_all>. Required.
11710: 
11711: =back
11712: 
11713: =head3 Returns
11714: 
11715: A string containing the full validation report, or a single line
11716: confirming all methods passed if no disagreements were found.
11717: 
11718: =head3 Side effects
11719: 
11720: None.
11721: 
11722: =head3 Notes
11723: 
11724: Only methods whose schemas contain a C<_pod_validation_errors> key
11725: (populated when C<strict_pod> is 1 or 2) appear in the report. If
11726: C<strict_pod> was 0 when C<extract_all> was called, this method will
11727: always return the all-passed message.
11728: 
11729: =head3 API specification
11730: 
11731: =head4 input
11732: 
11733:     {
11734:         self    => { type => OBJECT,  isa => 'App::Test::Generator::SchemaExtractor' },
11735:         schemas => { type => HASHREF },
11736:     }
11737: 
11738: =head4 output
11739: 
11740:     { type => SCALAR }
11741: 
11742: =cut
11743: 
11744: sub generate_pod_validation_report {
●11745 → 11748 → 1176011745: 	my ($self, $schemas) = @_;
11746: 
11747: 	my @reports;
11748: 	foreach my $method_name (sort keys %$schemas) {
11749: 		my $schema = $schemas->{$method_name};
11750: 
11751: 		if (my $errors = $schema->{_pod_validation_errors}) {
11752: 			push @reports, "Method: $method_name";
11753: 			push @reports, "  Severity: " . ($schema->{_pod_disagreement} ? 'warning' : 'fatal');
11754: 			push @reports, "  Errors:";
11755: 			push @reports, map { "    - $_" } @$errors;
11756: 			push @reports, '';
11757: 		}
11758: 	}
11759: 
●11760 → 11760 → 011760: 	if (@reports) {
11761: 		return join("\n", "POD/Code Validation Report:", '=' x 40, '', @reports);
11762: 	} else {
11763: 		return 'POD/Code Validation: All methods passed consistency checks.';
11764: 	}
11765: }
11766: 
11767: =head2 _log
11768: 
11769: Log a message if verbose mode is on.
11770: 
11771: =cut
11772: 
11773: sub _log {
11774: 	my($self, $msg) = @_;
11775: 
11776: 	print "$msg\n" if $self->{verbose};
11777: }
11778: 
11779: =head1 NOTES
11780: 
11781: C<SchemaExtractor> uses heuristic analysis of Perl source and POD to infer
11782: parameter types and constraints. Inference accuracy improves with
11783: well-documented modules; C<=head3 Input> / C<=head4 Input> formal specs are
11784: parsed at highest priority and override all heuristics.
11785: 
11786: A lone name in a C<=head4 input> block is treated as the first positional
11787: argument when the body reads a positional argument (C<< $_[0] >>, a list
11788: assignment from C<@_>, or a C<shift>) and does not read an argument hash
11789: (C<my %args = @_>, a hashref dereference, or C<< $_[0]{...} >>); the
11790: documented name is then a label for that argument, as C<name> is for
11791: C<$_[0]> in L<Lingua::EN::NameCase/nc>.  Two or more names, and a body that
11792: only forwards C<@_>, remain named arguments.
11793: 
11794: The output is always a best-effort schema suitable as a starting template;
11795: review and augment the generated YAML before using it as a definitive
11796: specification. Pass it to L<App::Test::Generator> to generate fuzz harnesses.
11797: 
11798: =head1 TODO
11799:

11801: synonym (union types, e.g. C<scalar | scalarref>, are already handled by
11802: C<_map_formal_input_type>).
11803:

11805:

11808: =item * L<App::Test::Generator> - Generate fuzz and corpus-driven test harnesses
11809:

11811: So with well-documented code, you can automatically create your tests.
